Let me write `notifyPairEvent`:

```ts
export function notifyPairEvent(
    world: World,
    entity: Entity,
    relationTrait: Trait,
    target: Entity,
    eventType: EventType
) {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const instance = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, relationTrait);
    if (!instance) return;
    const { generationId, bitflag } = instance;

    // Global event log for populating new queries
    logPairEvent(world, relationTrait, entity, target, eventType);

    for (const query of instance.trackingQueries) {
        const match =
            query.relationFilters && query.relationFilters.length > 0
                ? checkQueryTrackingWithRelations(world, query, entity, eventType, generationId, bitflag, target)
                : query.checkTracking(world, entity, eventType, generationId, bitflag, target);
        if (match) query.add(entity);
        else query.remove(world, entity);
    }
}
```

But wait — should we iterate ALL trackingQueries of the trait? That includes queries tracking the trait at trait-level too (e.g., `Added(ChildOf)`).
For those, passing target would cause pair tracker updates, but the group has no pairs, so no effect. The trait-level tracker update would happen in
`checkQueryTracking` because eventType matches and groupBitmask includes bit. So calling notifyPairEvent for trait-level queries also updates trait
trackers! That could double-count for the first/last pair add/remove: `addTraitToEntity` already updated the trait tracker; notifyPairEvent would
update it again. Since it's a bitmask OR, double OR is harmless (bit already set). For remove, the trait-level remove event sets bit; pair event ORs
same bit again. Harmless. For changed, `markChanged` already updated changedMask and per-query trackers; notifyPairEvent would set again. Harmless.

target would call `checkQueryTracking` with `'remove'`, which for the trait-level `Removed(Likes)` group (no pairs) would set the trait tracker bit
even though the trait wasn't removed (entity still has it). That would falsely mark `Removed(Likes)` as matching for a non-last pair removal! Wait —
would it? Let's trace: entity has Likes(alice) and Likes(bob). `Removed(Likes)` query group has bitmask for Likes, no pairs. Entity removes
Likes(alice) (non-last). `notifyPairEvent` fires with `'remove'`, genId/bitflag for Likes. `checkQueryTracking` sees group type 'remove', eventType
matches, groupBitmask & bitflag → updates tracker bit. Satisfaction: AND group with no pairs → traitMatched requires tracker has bit → true. So entity
matches `Removed(Likes)` even though it still has Likes! That's wrong.

So `notifyPairEvent` must NOT update trait-level trackers for queries whose group has no pairs. Or more precisely, pair events should only affect pair
trackers, not trait trackers. But `checkQueryTracking` currently conflates.

Let me reconsider. In `checkQueryTracking`, cross-event invalidation:
```ts

    if (groupType === 'add' || groupType === 'change') return false;
```

This fires whenever `groupBitmask & eventBitflag`, i.e., any add/remove of the base trait. For pair groups, we want this only if the event target

should match. The fact trait tracker got set by bob earlier is harmless. But cross-event invalidation by bob's removal would be wrong.

So let me make cross-event invalidation target-aware for pair groups:
- Determine if the event target matches any pair in the group (`pairMatchesEvent`).
- If group has pairs AND the event target does NOT match any pair, skip cross-event invalidation and skip trait tracker update for this group (because
the event is irrelevant to this group's tracked pairs).

    24	function createSoAFastSetFunction(schema: Schema) {
    25	    const keys = Object.keys(schema);
    26	
    27	    // Generate a hardcoded set function based on the schema keys
    28	    const setFunctionBody = keys.map((key) => `store.${key}[index] = value.${key};`).join('\n    ');
    29	
    30	    // Use new Function to create a set function with hardcoded keys
    31	    const set = new Function(
    32	        'index',
    33	        'store',
    34	        'value',
    35	        `
    36			${setFunctionBody}
    37		  `
    38	    );
    39	
    40	    return set;
    41	}
    42	
    43	// Return true if any trait value were changed.
    44	function createSoAFastSetChangeFunction(schema: Schema) {
    45	    const keys = Object.keys(schema);
    46	
    47	    // Generate a hardcoded set function based on the schema keys
    48	    const setFunctionBody = keys
    49	        .map(
    50	            (key) =>
    51	                `if (store.${key}[index] !== value.${key}) {
    52	            store.${key}[index] = value.${key};
    53	            changed = true;
    54	        }`
    55	        )
    56	        .join('\n    ');
    57	
    58	    // Use new Function to create a set function with hardcoded keys
    59	    const set = new Function(
    60	        'index',
    61	        'store',
    62	        'value',
    63	        `
    64	        let changed = false;
    65	        ${setFunctionBody}
    66	        return changed;
    67	        `
    68	    );
    69	
    70	    return set;
    71	}
    72	
    73	function createSoAGetFunction(schema: Schema) {
    74	    const keys = Object.keys(schema);
    75	
    76	    // Create an object literal with all keys assigned from the store
    77	    const objectLiteral = `{ ${keys.map((key) => `${key}: store.${key}[index]`).join(', ')} }`;
    78	
    79	    // Use new Function to create a get function that returns the pre-populated object
    80	    const get = new Function(
    81	        'index',
    82	        'store',
    83	        `
    84	        return ${objectLiteral};
    85	        `
    86	    );
    87	
    88	    return get;
    89	}
    90	
    91	function createAoSSetFunction(_schema: Schema) {
    92	    return (index: number, store: any, value: any) => {
    93	        store[index] = value;
    94	    };
    95	}
    96	
    97	function createAoSFastSetChangeFunction(_schema: Schema) {
    98	    return (index: number, store: any, value: any) => {
    99	        let changed = false;
   100	        if (value !== store[index]) {
   101	            store[index] = value;
   102	            changed = true;
   103	        }
   104	        return changed;
   105	    };
   106	}
   107	
   108	function createAoSGetFunction(_schema: Schema) {
   109	    return (index: number, store: any) => store[index];
   110	}

Let me design `PairTracker`:
```ts
type PairTrackerState = {
    delta: number;
    changed: boolean;
    data: unknown; // last data associated with this pair (set on add/change/remove)
};
```

When we log a pair event globally and update per-query pair trackers, we also set `data`.

For add: after adding, `data = getRelationData(world, entity, relation, target)`.
For change: after setting, `data = getRelationData(world, entity, relation, target)`.
For remove: before removing, `data = getRelationData(world, entity, relation, target)`.

Then at query iteration, for a pair-tracked trait with target:
- If entity currently has the pair → use `getRelationData` (current data).
- Else if there's a pair event state with `data` (from global log or per-query) → use that.
- Else undefined.

But `createSnapshots` would need access to the pair event state. We can pass the query's pair trackers or the global events. For readEach on a query
result, we have `query`. So we can access `query.trackingGroups[*].pairTrackers` and global events. But which group? We need a map from trait+target
to data. Simpler: in `createQueryResult`, build a `pairDataResolvers` array from params, and for each resolver, when snapshotting, look up data:
1. Try `getRelationData` (current).
2. If undefined/null (pair gone), look in the query's pair trackers for a state with data for that trait+target.
3. If not found, look in global `world[$internal].pairTrackingEvents` for any id... but which id? The resolver came from a modifier with a specific
id. We can store the modifier id in the resolver.

Let me define resolver:
```ts
type PairDataResolver = {
    relation: Relation<Trait>;
    target: Entity; // specific only (wildcard target for data doesn't make sense)
    modifierId?: number; // tracking id to look up event data
};
```

data for A remains in global events. For a query `Removed(Likes(alice))`, after drain, data for alice remains. If query runs again (no new events),

Now, **query population seeding from global events**. Let me implement in `createQueryInstance` population loop:

```ts
for (const group of query.trackingGroups) {
    const { type, id, logic, bitmasks } = group;
    const snapshot = ctx.trackingSnapshots.get(id)!;
    const dirtyMask = ctx.dirtyMasks.get(id)!;
    const changedMask = ctx.changedMasks.get(id)!;
    const globalPairEvents = ctx.pairTrackingEvents.get(id);

    for (const entity of ctx.entityIndex.dense) {
        ...
        // existing trait matching logic...
        // For pairs: check global pair events
        if (group.pairs.length) {
            // seed group.pairTrackers from globalPairEvents for this entity
            seedPairTrackersFromGlobal(group, eid, globalPairEvents, type);
            // then include pair satisfaction in `matches`
        }
        ...
    }
}
```

But the existing population loop's `matches` computation is complex with AND/OR. I need to integrate pair satisfaction. Let me refactor the population
loop to reuse a `checkGroupMatch` function that computes both trait and pair satisfaction.

Actually, the population loop and `checkQueryTracking` have duplicated satisfaction logic. Let me create a shared helper
`checkTrackingGroupMatch(world, group, eid, eventType?)` that:
- computes traitMatched from group.trackers
- computes pairMatched from group.pairTrackers (optionally seeding from global first)
- combines per logic.

Then both `checkQueryTracking` and population loop use it. But in `checkQueryTracking`, the trait tracker update happens before satisfaction; pair
update too. In population loop, we just check.

Hmm, but there's a difference: `checkQueryTracking` checks a single event and may early-return false on cross-event invalidation. The population loop
checks the full accumulated state. Let me write the shared helper for satisfaction:

```ts
function trackingGroupSatisfied(group, eid): boolean {
    const groupLogic = group.logic;
    // traits
    let traitMatched = groupLogic === 'and';
    const groupTrackers = group.trackers;
    const bitmasks = group.bitmasks;
    if (groupLogic === 'or') {
        for (let genId = 0; genId < bitmasks.length; genId++) {
            const mask = bitmasks[genId];
            if (!mask) continue;
            const trackerArr = groupTrackers[genId];
            const tracker = trackerArr ? (trackerArr[eid] | 0) : 0;
            if (tracker & mask) { traitMatched = true; break; }
        }
    } else {
        traitMatched = true;
        for (let genId = 0; genId < bitmasks.length; genId++) {
            const mask = bitmasks[genId];
            if (!mask) continue;
            const trackerArr = groupTrackers[genId];
            const tracker = trackerArr ? (trackerArr[eid] | 0) : 0;
            if ((tracker & mask) !== mask) { traitMatched = false; break; }
        }
    }
    // pairs
    let pairMatched = groupLogic === 'or' ? false : true;
    if (group.pairs.length) {
        const relationMap = group.pairTrackers.get(eid);
        if (groupLogic === 'and') {
            pairMatched = true;
            for (const pair of group.pairs) {
                if (!pairStateMatches(relationMap, pair, group.type)) { pairMatched = false; break; }
            }
        } else {
            pairMatched = false;
            for (const pair of group.pairs) {
                if (pairStateMatches(relationMap, pair, group.type)) { pairMatched = true; break; }
            }
        }
    }
    return groupLogic === 'and' ? (traitMatched && pairMatched) : (traitMatched || pairMatched);
}
```

Where `pairStateMatches` checks delta/changed.

Now `checkQueryTracking`:
- updates trait trackers for matching events
- updates pair trackers for matching pair events
- then for each group, computes satisfaction:
  - For OR groups: `if (trackingGroupSatisfied(group, eid)) anyOrMatched = true;`
  - For AND groups: `if (!trackingGroupSatisfied(group, eid)) return false;`
- returns `!hasOrGroup || anyOrMatched`.

And the population loop similarly computes satisfaction per group, plus applies relationFilters (top-level) at the end.

Now, in `checkQueryTracking`, we also need the target-aware pair matching for cross-event invalidation and pair updates. Let me write a helper
`groupAffectedByPairEvent(group, ctx, eventGenId, eventBitflag, target)`:
```ts
function groupAffectedByPairEvent(group, ctx, eventGenId, eventBitflag, target): boolean {
    if (!group.pairs.length) return false;
    for (const pair of group.pairs) {
        const pairTrait = pair[$internal].relation[$internal].trait;
        const inst = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, pairTrait);
        if (!inst) continue;
        if (inst.generationId === eventGenId && (inst.bitflag & eventBitflag)) {
            const pairTarget = pair[$internal].target;
            if (pairTarget === '*' || pairTarget === target) return true;
        }
    }
    return false;
}
```

Now, in `checkQueryTracking`, the event handling:

```ts
for (let i = 0; i < trackingGroupsLen; i++) {
    const group = trackingGroups[i];
    const groupType = group.type;
    const groupLogic = group.logic;
    const groupBitmasks = group.bitmasks;
    const groupBitmask = groupBitmasks[eventGenerationId];
    const groupTrackers = group.trackers;
    const groupPairs = group.pairs;

    // Determine if this event affects the group's tracked traits
    const affectsTrait = groupBitmask && (groupBitmask & eventBitflag);

    if (affectsTrait) {
        let applyCrossInvalidation = true;
        if (groupPairs.length > 0) {
            if (target !== undefined) {
                // pair event: only if target matches one of group's pairs
                if (!groupAffectedByPairEvent(group, ctx, eventGenerationId, eventBitflag, target)) {
                    applyCrossInvalidation = false;
                    // still update trait trackers? No - skip this group entirely for event processing
                } else {
                    // apply cross invalidation and update trait tracker + pair tracker
                }
            } else {
                // trait-level event (no target): applies to regular traits in group
                // For pair groups, a trait-level add/remove of the base trait should still count.
                // e.g., first pair add -> trait add; last pair remove -> trait remove.
            }
        }
        if (applyCrossInvalidation) {
            // cross-event invalidation
            if (eventType === 'remove') {
                if (groupType === 'add' || groupType === 'change') return false;
            } else if (eventType === 'add') {
                if (groupType === 'remove' || groupType === 'change') return false;
            }
            // update trait tracker if event type matches
            if (groupType === eventType) {
                // change verification...
                // set tracker bit
            }
        }
    }

    // update pair tracker if pair event matches
    if (target !== undefined && groupPairs.length && groupType === eventType) {
        if (groupAffectedByPairEvent(group, ctx, eventGenerationId, eventBitflag, target)) {
            updatePairTracker(group, eid, relationTraitId, targetId, eventType, data);
        }
    }

    // satisfaction...
}
```

Wait, there's a subtlety for pair groups when target is undefined (trait-level event for base trait). For `Added(Likes(alice))`, first pair add fires
trait-level add (target undefined). `affectsTrait` true (Likes bit). Since groupPairs.length > 0 and target undefined, we go to the trait-level
branch. Should we apply cross-invalidation and update trait tracker? For an add event of Likes on an 'add' group: no invalidation, update trait
tracker. Good. For a remove event of Likes on an 'add' group (last pair removed): cross-invalidation → return false. Good.

But here's the issue: a trait-level remove of Likes fires only when the LAST pair is removed. For `Added(Likes(alice))`, if the entity added alice
then removed alice (last), the trait-level remove would invalidate. And the pair-level remove (target alice) would also invalidate. Both same target,
consistent. If entity added alice, added bob, then removed bob (non-last, no trait-level remove), pair-level remove for bob fires. For
`Added(Likes(alice))`, groupAffectedByPairEvent for bob → false (pair target alice), so skip → entity stays in Added(alice). Good. For
`Added(Likes(bob))`, pair remove for bob → true → invalidate → removed. Good.

Now, what about a trait-level ADD of Likes for `Removed(Likes(alice))` on first pair add? target undefined, affectsTrait true, groupPairs.length>0,
target undefined → trait-level branch. Cross-invalidation: eventType 'add', groupType 'remove' → return false. Good (the add invalidates Removed).
Then pair add for alice also → groupAffectedByPairEvent true → cross-invalidation return false. Good.

Now, what about a trait-level ADD of Likes for `Added(Likes(alice))` where the group has pair target alice — should we update the trait tracker? Yes,
in trait-level branch, groupType === eventType → set tracker bit. But then pair event for alice also sets it (harmless). Fine.

However, there's a tricky case: `Added(Likes(alice), Position)` group has pairs [Likes(alice)] and bitmasks include both Likes and Position. When
Position is added (trait event, target undefined), affectsTrait true (Position bit), groupPairs.length>0 and target undefined → trait-level branch. It
updates Position tracker. Good. When Likes(alice) is added as non-first pair (pair event target alice), affectsTrait true (Likes bit),
groupPairs.length>0 and target defined → groupAffectedByPairEvent true → update trait tracker (Likes bit) and pair tracker. Both set. Satisfaction:
traitMatched requires both Likes and Position trackers set; pairMatched requires Likes(alice) delta>0. All set → match. Good.

But wait: when Likes(alice) added as FIRST pair, `addTraitToEntity` fires trait-level add (target undefined) for Likes → trait branch updates Likes
tracker. Then pair event updates pair tracker. Good.

Now, what about `Added(Likes(alice))` when a DIFFERENT regular trait in a different group (e.g., query `Added(Likes(alice)), Removed(Position)`) —
Position removal event target undefined. For group Added(Likes(alice)): affectsTrait? Position bit not in group bitmask → false. No effect. Good.

Now, there's one more subtlety: for pair groups, when a trait-level event fires for the base trait (target undefined), we said apply normal
cross-invalidation and trait update. But what if the group has pairs AND the event is a trait-level add of the base trait because the entity got the
trait through a non-pair path? Relation traits are only added via addRelationPair (which also fires pair event), so base trait add always corresponds
to first pair. Fine.

Now let's think about **`Changed(Likes(alice))`**:
- `setPairChanged` → `markChanged(world, entity, trait, target)`.
- `markChanged` sets changedMasks bit globally and iterates tracking queries. It calls `checkQueryTracking` with target. For group
`Changed(Likes(alice))`, target defined, groupAffectedByPairEvent true → cross-invalidation: eventType 'change', groupType 'change' → no invalidation.
Update trait tracker? For 'change', checkQueryTracking's trait update path: if groupType === eventType → verify entity has trait → set tracker bit.
Then pair update: set changed=true. Satisfaction: traitMatched (changed bit) AND pairMatched (changed true) → match. Good.
- But `markChanged` is also called by `setChanged` for regular trait changes (no target). For `Changed(Likes(alice))` when entity.set(Likes)
(trait-level set, not pair) — `setTraitForTrait` calls `setChanged` with no target. In checkQueryTracking, target undefined, groupPairs.length>0 →
trait-level branch. For group `Changed(Likes(alice))`, eventType 'change' matches → trait tracker updated, but pair tracker not. Satisfaction:
pairMatched false → no match. Good — trait-level set of relation trait without target doesn't count as changing a specific pair? But actually
`setTrait(Likes, ...)` (without pair) sets the whole base trait store (array form), which is unusual. Fine.

Now, **global pair event logging**. Let me define in world types. Let me look at world/types.ts.

export const $internal = Symbol.for('koota.internal');

/**
 * Type utility for symbol-branded runtime type checks.
 * Allows accessing a symbol property while maintaining type safety.
 */
export type Brand<S extends symbol> = { readonly [K in S]?: true };
---
import type { Entity } from '../types';

// Constants for bit sizes
export const WORLD_ID_BITS = 4; // 4 bits can represent 0-15
export const GENERATION_BITS = 8; // 8 bits can represent 0-255
export const ENTITY_ID_BITS = 20; // 20 bits can represent 0-1,048,575
// Total bits used: 4 + 8 + 20 = 32 bits

// Masks for extracting values
export const WORLD_ID_MASK = (1 << WORLD_ID_BITS) - 1;
export const GENERATION_MASK = (1 << GENERATION_BITS) - 1;
export const ENTITY_ID_MASK = (1 << ENTITY_ID_BITS) - 1;

// Bit shifts for positioning each component
export const GENERATION_SHIFT = ENTITY_ID_BITS;
export const WORLD_ID_SHIFT = GENERATION_SHIFT + GENERATION_BITS;

export function packEntity(worldId: number, generation: number, entityId: number): Entity {
    return (((worldId & WORLD_ID_MASK) << WORLD_ID_SHIFT) |
        ((generation & GENERATION_MASK) << GENERATION_SHIFT) |
        (entityId & ENTITY_ID_MASK)) as Entity;
}

export function unpackEntity(entity: Entity) {
    return {
        worldId: entity >>> WORLD_ID_SHIFT,
        generation: (entity >>> GENERATION_SHIFT) & GENERATION_MASK,
        entityId: entity & ENTITY_ID_MASK,
    };
}

export const getEntityId = /* @inline @pure */ (entity: Entity) => entity & ENTITY_ID_MASK;
export const getEntityWorldId = /* @inline @pure */ (entity: Entity) => entity >>> WORLD_ID_SHIFT;
export const getEntityAndWorldId = /* @pure */ (entity: Entity): [number, number] => [
    entity & ENTITY_ID_MASK,
    entity >>> WORLD_ID_SHIFT,
];

export const getEntityGeneration = /* @inline @pure */ (entity: Entity) =>
    (entity >>> GENERATION_SHIFT) & GENERATION_MASK;

export const incrementGeneration = (entity: Entity): Entity =>
    ((entity & ~(GENERATION_MASK << GENERATION_SHIFT)) | // Clear current generation bits
        (((((entity >>> GENERATION_SHIFT) & GENERATION_MASK) + 1) & GENERATION_MASK) <<
            GENERATION_SHIFT)) as unknown as Entity; // Extract generation, increment, wrap around, shift back, and combine

import { Brand } from '../common';
import { Trait } from '../trait/types';
import { EventType, Modifier, OrModifier, QueryParameter } from './types';

export const $modifier = Symbol('modifier');

export function createModifier<TTrait extends Trait[] = Trait[], TType extends string = string>(
    type: TType,
    id: number,
    traits: TTrait
): Modifier<TTrait, TType> {
    return {
        [$modifier]: true,
        type,
        id,
        traits,
        traitIds: traits.map((trait) => trait.id),
    } as const;
}

export /* @inline @pure */ function isModifier(param: QueryParameter): param is Modifier {
    return (param as Brand<typeof $modifier> | null | undefined)?.[$modifier] as unknown as boolean;
}

/** Check if a modifier is a tracking modifier (added, removed, or changed) */
export function isTrackingModifier(modifier: Modifier): boolean {
    const { type } = modifier;
    return type.includes('added') || type.includes('removed') || type.includes('changed');
}

/** Get the tracking type from a modifier */
export function getTrackingType(modifier: Modifier): EventType | null {
    const { type } = modifier;
    if (type.includes('added')) return 'add';
    if (type.includes('removed')) return 'remove';
    if (type.includes('changed')) return 'change';
    return null;
}

/** Check if an Or modifier has nested modifiers */
export function isOrWithModifiers(modifier: Modifier): modifier is OrModifier {
    return modifier.type === 'or' && Array.isArray((modifier as OrModifier).modifiers);
}

import { $internal } from '../../common';
import { isRelation } from '../../relation/utils/is-relation';
import type { ExtractTraits, TraitOrRelation } from '../../trait/types';
import { universe } from '../../universe/universe';
import { createModifier } from '../modifier';
import type { Modifier } from '../types';
import { createTrackingId, setTrackingMasks } from '../utils/tracking-cursor';

export function createAdded() {
    const id = createTrackingId();

    for (const world of universe.worlds) {
        if (!world) continue;
        setTrackingMasks(world, id);
    }

    return <T extends TraitOrRelation[]>(
        ...inputs: T
    ): Modifier<ExtractTraits<T>, `added-${number}`> => {
        const traits = inputs.map((input) =>
            isRelation(input) ? input[$internal].trait : input
        ) as ExtractTraits<T>;
        return createModifier(`added-${id}`, id, traits);
    };
}
import { $internal } from '../../common';
import { isRelation } from '../../relation/utils/is-relation';
import type { ExtractTraits, TraitOrRelation } from '../../trait/types';
import { universe } from '../../universe/universe';
import { createModifier } from '../modifier';
import type { Modifier } from '../types';
import { createTrackingId, setTrackingMasks } from '../utils/tracking-cursor';

export function createRemoved() {
    const id = createTrackingId();

    for (const world of universe.worlds) {
        if (!world) continue;
        setTrackingMasks(world, id);
    }

    return <T extends TraitOrRelation[]>(
        ...inputs: T
    ): Modifier<ExtractTraits<T>, `removed-${number}`> => {
        const traits = inputs.map((input) =>
            isRelation(input) ? input[$internal].trait : input
        ) as ExtractTraits<T>;
        return createModifier(`removed-${id}`, id, traits);
    };
}

```ts
export function resolveTrackingInputs(inputs: (Trait | Relation<Trait> | RelationPair)[]) {
    const traits: Trait[] = [];
    const pairs: RelationPair[] = [];
    for (const input of inputs) {
        if (isRelationPair(input)) {
            pairs.push(input);
            traits.push(input[$internal].relation[$internal].trait);
        } else if (isRelation(input)) {
            traits.push(input[$internal].trait);
        } else {
            traits.push(input);
        }
    }
    return { traits, pairs };
}
```

/** Resolve tracking modifier inputs (traits, relations, relation pairs) into base traits + pairs. */
export function resolveTrackingInputs(
    inputs: (Trait | import('../relation/types').Relation<Trait> | RelationPair)[]
): { traits: Trait[]; pairs: RelationPair[] } {
    const traits: Trait[] = [];
    const pairs: RelationPair[] = [];

    for (const input of inputs) {
        if (isRelationPair(input)) {
            pairs.push(input);
            traits.push(input[$internal].relation[$internal].trait);
        } else if (isRelation(input)) {
            traits.push(input[$internal].trait);
        } else {
            traits.push(input);
        }
    }

    return { traits, pairs };
}
"""

import { Brand } from '../common';
import { isRelation, isRelationPair } from '../relation/utils/is-relation';
import type { RelationPair } from '../relation/types';
import { Trait } from '../trait/types';
import { EventType, Modifier, OrModifier, QueryParameter } from './types';

export const $modifier = Symbol('modifier');

export function createModifier<TTrait extends Trait[] = Trait[], TType extends string = string>(
    type: TType,
    id: number,
    traits: TTrait,
    pairs?: RelationPair[]
): Modifier<TTrait, TType> {
    return {
        [$modifier]: true,
        type,
        id,
        traits,
        traitIds: traits.map((trait) => trait.id),
        ...(pairs && pairs.length > 0 ? { pairs } : {}),
    } as const;
}

export /* @inline @pure */ function isModifier(param: QueryParameter): param is Modifier {
    return (param as Brand<typeof $modifier> | null | undefined)?.[$modifier] as unknown as boolean;
}

/** Check if a modifier is a tracking modifier (added, removed, or changed) */
export function isTrackingModifier(modifier: Modifier): boolean {
    const { type } = modifier;
    return type.includes('added') || type.includes('removed') || type.includes('changed');
}

/** Get the tracking type from a modifier */
export function getTrackingType(modifier: Modifier): EventType | null {
    const { type } = modifier;
    if (type.includes('added')) return 'add';
    if (type.includes('removed')) return 'remove';
    if (type.includes('changed')) return 'change';
    return null;
}

/** Check if an Or modifier has nested modifiers */
export function isOrWithModifiers(modifier: Modifier): modifier is OrModifier {
    return modifier.type === 'or' && Array.isArray((modifier as OrModifier).modifiers);
}

/** Resolve tracking modifier inputs (traits, relations, relation pairs) into base traits + pairs. */
export function resolveTrackingInputs(
    inputs: (Trait | import('../relation/types').Relation<Trait> | RelationPair)[]
): { traits: Trait[]; pairs: RelationPair[] } {
    const traits: Trait[] = [];
    const pairs: RelationPair[] = [];

    for (const input of inputs) {
        if (isRelationPair(input)) {
            pairs.push(input);
            traits.push(input[$internal].relation[$internal].trait);
        } else if (isRelation(input)) {
            traits.push(input[$internal].trait);
        } else {
            traits.push(input);
        }
    }

    return { traits, pairs };
}

import { $internal } from '../../common';
import type { Entity } from '../../entity/types';
import { getEntityId } from '../../entity/utils/pack-entity';
import { isRelation } from '../../relation/utils/is-relation';
import { hasTrait, registerTrait } from '../../trait/trait';
import { getTraitInstance, hasTraitInstance } from '../../trait/trait-instance';
import type { ExtractTraits, Trait, TraitOrRelation } from '../../trait/types';
import { universe } from '../../universe/universe';
import type { World } from '../../world';
import { createModifier } from '../modifier';
import type { Modifier } from '../types';
import { checkQueryTrackingWithRelations } from '../utils/check-query-tracking-with-relations';
import { createTrackingId, setTrackingMasks } from '../utils/tracking-cursor';

export function createChanged() {
    const id = createTrackingId();

    for (const world of universe.worlds) {
        if (!world) continue;
        setTrackingMasks(world, id);
    }

    return <T extends TraitOrRelation[]>(
        ...inputs: T
    ): Modifier<ExtractTraits<T>, `changed-${number}`> => {
        const traits = inputs.map((input) =>
            isRelation(input) ? input[$internal].trait : input
        ) as ExtractTraits<T>;
        return createModifier(`changed-${id}`, id, traits);
    };
}

/** @inline */
function markChanged(world: World, entity: Entity, trait: Trait) {
    const ctx = world[$internal];

    // Early exit if the trait is not on the entity.
    if (!hasTrait(world, entity, trait)) return;

    // Register the trait if it's not already registered.
    if (!hasTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, trait)) registerTrait(world, trait);
    const data = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, trait)!;

    // Mark the trait as changed in bitmasks for Changed modifiers.
    const eid = getEntityId(entity);
    const { generationId, bitflag } = data;

    for (const changedMask of ctx.changedMasks.values()) {
        if (!changedMask[generationId]) changedMask[generationId] = [];
        if (!changedMask[generationId][eid]) changedMask[generationId][eid] = 0;
        changedMask[generationId][eid] |= bitflag;
    }

    // Update tracking queries with change event
    for (const query of data.trackingQueries) {
        if (!query.hasChangedModifiers) continue;
        if (!query.changedTraits.has(trait)) continue;

        const match =
            query.relationFilters && query.relationFilters.length > 0
                ? checkQueryTrackingWithRelations(
                      world,
                      query,
                      entity,
                      'change',
                      generationId,
                      bitflag
                  )
                : query.checkTracking(world, entity, 'change', generationId, bitflag);
        if (match) query.add(entity);
        else query.remove(world, entity);
    }

    return data;
}

export function setChanged(world: World, entity: Entity, trait: Trait) {
    const data = markChanged(world, entity, trait);
    if (!data) return;
    for (const sub of data.changeSubscriptions) sub(entity);
}

export function setPairChanged(world: World, entity: Entity, trait: Trait, target: Entity) {
    const data = markChanged(world, entity, trait);
    if (!data) return;
    for (const sub of data.changeSubscriptions) sub(entity, target);
}

cd /app && cat > packages/core/src/query/utils/pair-tracking.ts <<'EOF'
import { $internal } from '../../common';
import type { Entity } from '../../entity/types';
import { getEntityId } from '../../entity/utils/pack-entity';
import { getRelationData } from '../../relation/relation';
import type { Relation, RelationPair } from '../../relation/types';
import { getTraitInstance } from '../../trait/trait-instance';
import type { Trait } from '../../trait/types';
import type { World } from '../../world';
import type { EventType, PairTrackerMap, PairTrackerState, QueryInstance } from '../types';
import { checkQueryTrackingWithRelations } from './check-query-tracking-with-relations';

/**
 * Update (or create) the per-target pair tracker state for a pair event.
 */
export function updatePairTracker(
    map: PairTrackerMap,
    eid: number,
    relationTraitId: number,
    targetId: number,
    eventType: EventType,
    data?: unknown
): PairTrackerState {
    let relationMap = map.get(eid);
    if (!relationMap) {
        relationMap = new Map();
        map.set(eid, relationMap);
    }
    let targetMap = relationMap.get(relationTraitId);
    if (!targetMap) {
        targetMap = new Map();
        relationMap.set(relationTraitId, targetMap);
    }
    let state = targetMap.get(targetId);
    if (!state) {
        state = { delta: 0, changed: false };
        targetMap.set(targetId, state);
    }

    if (eventType === 'add') state.delta++;
    else if (eventType === 'remove') state.delta--;
    else if (eventType === 'change') state.changed = true;

    if (data !== undefined) state.data = data;

    return state;
}

/**
 * Check whether a tracked relation pair has a matching event state for the given event type.
 * A wildcard target ('*') matches any target recorded for that relation trait.
 */
export function pairStateMatches(
    map: PairTrackerMap | undefined,
    eid: number,
    pair: RelationPair,
    eventType: EventType
): boolean {
    if (!map) return false;
    const relationMap = map.get(eid);
    if (!relationMap) return false;

    const pairCtx = pair[$internal];
    const relationTrait = pairCtx.relation[$internal].trait;
    const targetMap = relationMap.get(relationTrait.id);
    if (!targetMap) return false;

    const pairTarget = pairCtx.target;

    if (pairTarget === '*') {
        for (const state of targetMap.values()) {
            if (eventType === 'add' && state.delta > 0) return true;
            if (eventType === 'remove' && state.delta < 0) return true;
            if (eventType === 'change' && state.changed) return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    if (typeof pairTarget !== 'number') return false;
    const state = targetMap.get(pairTarget);
    if (!state) return false;

    if (eventType === 'add' && state.delta > 0) return true;
    if (eventType === 'remove' && state.delta < 0) return true;
    if (eventType === 'change' && state.changed) return true;
    return false;
}

/**
 * Copy global pair events (since factory creation) into a query group's pair trackers.
 * Used when populating newly created query instances.
 */
export function seedPairTrackersFromGlobal(
    map: PairTrackerMap,
    globalEvents: PairTrackerMap | undefined,
    eid: number
): void {
    if (!globalEvents) return;
    const relationMap = globalEvents.get(eid);
    if (!relationMap) return;
    for (const [relationTraitId, targetMap] of relationMap) {
        for (const [targetId, state] of targetMap) {
            updatePairTracker(map, eid, relationTraitId, targetId, state.delta > 0 ? 'add' : state.delta < 0 ? 'remove' : state.changed ? 'change' : 'add', state.data);
        }
    }
}

/**
 * Log a pair event into the world-level tracking log so newly created query
 * instances can be populated. Also records the per-target data for iteration.
 */
export function logPairEvent(
    world: World,
    relationTrait: Trait,
    entity: Entity,
    target: Entity,
    eventType: EventType,
    data?: unknown
): void {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const eid = getEntityId(entity);
    const targetId = getEntityId(target);

    for (const [id, events] of ctx.pairTrackingEvents) {
        void id;
        updatePairTracker(events, eid, relationTrait.id, targetId, eventType, data);
    }
}

/**
 * Notify all tracking queries that include a relation pair event.
 * Only queries with pair tracking are processed so trait-only queries do not
 * observe pair-level add/remove events (which would incorrectly match
 * non-first additions / non-last removals).
 */
export function notifyPairEvent(
    world: World,
    entity: Entity,
    relationTrait: Trait,
    target: Entity,
    eventType: EventType,
    data?: unknown
): void {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const instance = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, relationTrait);
    if (!instance) return;

    const { generationId, bitflag } = instance;

    // Log globally first so late-created queries can be populated.
    logPairEvent(world, relationTrait, entity, target, eventType, data);

    for (const query of instance.trackingQueries) {
        if (!query.hasPairTracking) continue;

        const match =
            query.relationFilters && query.relationFilters.length > 0
                ? checkQueryTrackingWithRelations(
                      world,
                      query,
                      entity,
                      eventType,
                      generationId,
                      bitflag,
                      target
                  )
                : query.checkTracking(world, entity, eventType, generationId, bitflag, target);
        if (match) query.add(entity);
        else query.remove(world, entity);
    }
}

/**
 * Get the data for a relation pair, used by query result iteration. Prefers live
 * data; falls back to the last recorded event data (needed for Removed pairs).
 */
export function getPairTrackedData(
    world: World,
    entity: Entity,
    relation: Relation<Trait>,
    target: Entity
): unknown {
    const data = getRelationData(world, entity, relation, target);
    if (data !== undefined) return data;

    // Fall back to pair event state (global log) for removed pairs.
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const eid = getEntityId(entity);
    const targetId = getEntityId(target);
    const relationTrait = relation[$internal].trait;

    for (const events of ctx.pairTrackingEvents.values()) {
        const relationMap = events.get(eid);
        if (!relationMap) continue;
        const targetMap = relationMap.get(relationTrait.id);
        if (!targetMap) continue;
        const state = targetMap.get(targetId);
        if (state && state.data !== undefined) return state.data;
    }

    return undefined;
}

    map: PairTrackerMap,

    eid: number

    if (!relationMap) return;
    for (const [relationTraitId, targetMap] of relationMap) {
        for (const [targetId, state] of targetMap) {
            updatePairTracker(map, eid, relationTraitId, targetId, state.delta > 0 ? 'add' : state.delta < 0 ? 'remove' : state.changed ? 'change' : 'add', state.data);

new="""export function seedPairTrackersFromGlobal(
    map: PairTrackerMap,
    globalEvents: PairTrackerMap | undefined,
    eid: number
): void {
    if (!globalEvents) return;
    const relationMap = globalEvents.get(eid);
    if (!relationMap) return;
    for (const [relationTraitId, targetMap] of relationMap) {
        for (const [targetId, state] of targetMap) {
            if (state.delta > 0) {
                updatePairTracker(map, eid, relationTraitId, targetId, 'add', state.data);
            } else if (state.delta < 0) {
                updatePairTracker(map, eid, relationTraitId, targetId, 'remove', state.data);
            } else if (state.changed) {
                updatePairTracker(map, eid, relationTraitId, targetId, 'change', state.data);
            }
        }
    }
}"""

cd /app && cat > packages/core/src/query/utils/check-query-tracking.ts <<'EOF'
import { $internal } from '../../common';
import { Entity } from '../../entity/types';
import { getEntityId } from '../../entity/utils/pack-entity';
import { getTraitInstance } from '../../trait/trait-instance';
import { World } from '../../world';
import { EventType, QueryInstance } from '../types';
import { pairStateMatches, updatePairTracker } from './pair-tracking';

/**
 * Check if a group's relation-pair filters are affected by a pair event.
 * A wildcard pair target matches any target for the same relation trait.
 */
function groupAffectedByPairEvent(
    query: QueryInstance,
    group: QueryInstance['trackingGroups'][number],
    world: World,
    eventGenerationId: number,
    eventBitflag: number,
    target: Entity
): boolean {
    if (group.pairs.length === 0) return false;
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const targetId = getEntityId(target);
    const pairs = group.pairs;
    const len = pairs.length;
    for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
        const pair = pairs[i];
        const pairCtx = pair[$internal];
        const pairTrait = pairCtx.relation[$internal].trait;
        const instance = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, pairTrait);
        if (!instance) continue;
        if (instance.generationId === eventGenerationId && (instance.bitflag & eventBitflag)) {
            const pairTarget = pairCtx.target;
            if (pairTarget === '*' || pairTarget === targetId) return true;
        }
    }
    void query;
    return false;
}

/**
 * Check if an entity matches a tracking query with event handling.
 *
 * PERF: This is a hot path - optimizations applied:
 * - Cache all property accesses at function start
 * - Use `| 0` instead of `|| 0` (bitwise coerces undefined to 0)
 * - Avoid optional chaining in inner loops
 * - Cache array references before mutation
 * - Early exits where possible
 */
export function checkQueryTracking(
    world: World,
    query: QueryInstance,
    entity: Entity,
    eventType: EventType,
    eventGenerationId: number,
    eventBitflag: number,
    target?: Entity
): boolean {
    // Cache all property accesses upfront
    const staticBitmasks = query.staticBitmasks;
    const trackingGroups = query.trackingGroups;
    const generations = query.generations;
    const traitInstancesAll = query.traitInstances.all;
    const entityMasks = world[$internal].entityMasks;
    const eid = getEntityId(entity);

    const generationsLen = generations.length;
    const trackingGroupsLen = trackingGroups.length;

    // Early exit: no traits to check
    if (traitInstancesAll.length === 0) return false;

    // 1. Check static constraints (required/forbidden/or)
    for (let i = 0; i < generationsLen; i++) {
        const generationId = generations[i];
        const bitmask = staticBitmasks[i];
        if (!bitmask) continue;

        const required = bitmask.required;
        const forbidden = bitmask.forbidden;
        const or = bitmask.or;

        // PERF: Direct access + bitwise OR coerces undefined to 0
        const genMasks = entityMasks[generationId];
        const entityMask = genMasks ? (genMasks[eid] | 0) : 0;

        // Check forbidden traits
        if (forbidden && (entityMask & forbidden) !== 0) return false;

        // Check required traits
        if (required && (entityMask & required) !== required) return false;

        // Check Or traits
        if (or !== 0 && (entityMask & or) === 0) return false;
    }

    const ctx = world[$internal];

    // 2. Process tracking groups - update trackers and check cross-event invalidation
    // Also track OR group state to avoid second loop when possible
    let hasOrGroup = false;
    let anyOrMatched = false;

    for (let i = 0; i < trackingGroupsLen; i++) {
        const group = trackingGroups[i];
        const groupType = group.type;
        const groupLogic = group.logic;
        const groupBitmasks = group.bitmasks;
        const groupBitmask = groupBitmasks[eventGenerationId];
        const groupTrackers = group.trackers;
        const groupPairs = group.pairs;

        // Determine if this event affects the group's tracked traits
        const affectsTrait = groupBitmask && (groupBitmask & eventBitflag);

        if (affectsTrait) {
            let shouldProcess = true;

            // For pair groups, a pair event must match one of the group's pair
            // filters; otherwise it's a different target and must not affect this
            // group (per-target reactivity).
            if (groupPairs.length > 0 && target !== undefined) {
                if (!groupAffectedByPairEvent(query, group, world, eventGenerationId, eventBitflag, target)) {
                    shouldProcess = false;
                }
            }

            if (shouldProcess) {
                // Cross-event invalidation:
                // - Remove event invalidates Added/Changed tracking
                // - Add event invalidates Removed/Changed tracking
                if (eventType === 'remove') {
                    if (groupType === 'add' || groupType === 'change') return false;
                } else if (eventType === 'add') {
                    if (groupType === 'remove' || groupType === 'change') return false;
                }

                // Update tracker if event type matches group type
                if (groupType === eventType) {
                    // For change events, verify entity still has the trait
                    if (eventType === 'change') {
                        const genMasks = entityMasks[eventGenerationId];
                        const entityMask = genMasks ? (genMasks[eid] | 0) : 0;
                        if (!(entityMask & eventBitflag)) return false;
                    }

                    // PERF: Cache tracker array reference before mutation
                    let trackerArr = groupTrackers[eventGenerationId];
                    if (!trackerArr) {
                        trackerArr = [];
                        groupTrackers[eventGenerationId] = trackerArr;
                    }
                    trackerArr[eid] = (trackerArr[eid] | 0) | eventBitflag;
                }
            }
        }

        // Update pair trackers if this is a pair event matching the group
        if (target !== undefined && groupPairs.length > 0 && groupType === eventType) {
            if (groupAffectedByPairEvent(query, group, world, eventGenerationId, eventBitflag, target)) {
                const targetId = getEntityId(target);
                // Find the relation trait id from the first matching pair
                const pair = groupPairs.find((p) => {
                    const pairCtx = p[$internal];
                    const pairTrait = pairCtx.relation[$internal].trait;
                    const instance = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, pairTrait);
                    if (!instance) return false;
                    if (instance.generationId !== eventGenerationId) return false;
                    if (!(instance.bitflag & eventBitflag)) return false;
                    const pairTarget = pairCtx.target;
                    return pairTarget === '*' || pairTarget === targetId;
                });
                if (pair) {
                    const relationTraitId = pair[$internal].relation[$internal].trait.id;
                    updatePairTracker(group.pairTrackers, eid, relationTraitId, targetId, eventType);
                }
            }
        }

        // 3. Verify tracking group satisfaction (merged into same loop)
        if (groupLogic === 'or') {
            hasOrGroup = true;
            if (!anyOrMatched) {
                // Check if any trait in OR group has been tracked
                const bitmaskLen = groupBitmasks.length;
                let traitMatched = false;
                for (let genId = 0; genId < bitmaskLen; genId++) {
                    const mask = groupBitmasks[genId];
                    if (!mask) continue;
                    const trackerArr = groupTrackers[genId];
                    const tracker = trackerArr ? (trackerArr[eid] | 0) : 0;
                    if (tracker & mask) {
                        traitMatched = true;
                        break;
                    }
                }

                // Check if any pair in OR group has been tracked
                let pairMatched = false;
                if (groupPairs.length > 0) {
                    for (const pair of groupPairs) {
                        if (pairStateMatches(group.pairTrackers, eid, pair, groupType)) {
                            pairMatched = true;
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                }

                if (traitMatched || pairMatched) anyOrMatched = true;
            }
        } else {
            // AND group: all traits must be tracked
            const bitmaskLen = groupBitmasks.length;
            let traitMatched = true;
            for (let genId = 0; genId < bitmaskLen; genId++) {
                const mask = groupBitmasks[genId];
                if (!mask) continue;
                const trackerArr = groupTrackers[genId];
                const tracker = trackerArr ? (trackerArr[eid] | 0) : 0;
                if ((tracker & mask) !== mask) {
                    traitMatched = false;
                    break;
                }
            }

            // AND group: all pairs must match
            let pairMatched = true;
            if (groupPairs.length > 0) {
                for (const pair of groupPairs) {
                    if (!pairStateMatches(group.pairTrackers, eid, pair, groupType)) {
                        pairMatched = false;
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }

            if (!traitMatched || !pairMatched) {
                return false;
            }
        }
    }

    // If we have OR groups, at least one must match
    if (hasOrGroup && !anyOrMatched) {
        return false;
    }

    return true;
}
EOF

import type { Entity } from '../../entity/types';
import { hasRelationPair } from '../../relation/relation';
import type { World } from '../../world';
import type { EventType, QueryInstance } from '../types';
import { checkQueryTracking } from './check-query-tracking';

/**
 * Check if an entity matches a tracking query with relation filters.
 * Combines checkQueryTracking (trait bitmasks + tracking state) with relation checks.
 */
export function checkQueryTrackingWithRelations(
    world: World,
    query: QueryInstance,
    entity: Entity,
    eventType: EventType,
    eventGenerationId: number,
    eventBitflag: number,
    target?: Entity
): boolean {
    // First check trait bitmasks and tracking state (fast)
    if (
        !checkQueryTracking(
            world,
            query,
            entity,
            eventType,
            eventGenerationId,
            eventBitflag,
            target
        )
    ) {
        return false;
    }

    // Then check relation pairs if any
    if (query.relationFilters && query.relationFilters.length > 0) {
        for (const pair of query.relationFilters) {
            if (!hasRelationPair(world, entity, pair)) {
                return false;
            }
        }
    }

    return true;
}

import { $internal } from '../../common';
import type { Entity } from '../../entity/types';
import { getEntityId } from '../../entity/utils/pack-entity';
import { getRelationData } from '../../relation/relation';
import { hasTrait, registerTrait } from '../../trait/trait';
import { getTraitInstance, hasTraitInstance } from '../../trait/trait-instance';
import type { ExtractTraits, Trait, TraitOrRelationOrPair } from '../../trait/types';
import { universe } from '../../universe/universe';
import type { World } from '../../world';
import { createModifier, resolveTrackingInputs } from '../modifier';
import type { Modifier } from '../types';
import { checkQueryTrackingWithRelations } from '../utils/check-query-tracking-with-relations';
import { logPairEvent } from '../utils/pair-tracking';
import { createTrackingId, setTrackingMasks } from '../utils/tracking-cursor';

export function createChanged() {
    const id = createTrackingId();

    for (const world of universe.worlds) {
        if (!world) continue;
        setTrackingMasks(world, id);
    }

    return <T extends TraitOrRelationOrPair[]>(
        ...inputs: T
    ): Modifier<ExtractTraits<T>, `changed-${number}`> => {
        const { traits, pairs } = resolveTrackingInputs(inputs);
        return createModifier(`changed-${id}`, id, traits as ExtractTraits<T>, pairs);
    };
}

/** @inline */
function markChanged(world: World, entity: Entity, trait: Trait, target?: Entity) {
    const ctx = world[$internal];

    // Early exit if the trait is not on the entity.
    if (!hasTrait(world, entity, trait)) return;

    // Register the trait if it's not already registered.
    if (!hasTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, trait)) registerTrait(world, trait);
    const data = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, trait)!;

    // Mark the trait as changed in bitmasks for Changed modifiers.
    const eid = getEntityId(entity);
    const { generationId, bitflag } = data;

    for (const changedMask of ctx.changedMasks.values()) {
        if (!changedMask[generationId]) changedMask[generationId] = [];
        if (!changedMask[generationId][eid]) changedMask[generationId][eid] = 0;
        changedMask[generationId][eid] |= bitflag;
    }

    // Update tracking queries with change event
    for (const query of data.trackingQueries) {
        if (!query.hasChangedModifiers) continue;
        if (!query.changedTraits.has(trait)) continue;

        const match =
            query.relationFilters && query.relationFilters.length > 0
                ? checkQueryTrackingWithRelations(
                      world,
                      query,
                      entity,
                      'change',
                      generationId,
                      bitflag,
                      target
                  )
                : query.checkTracking(world, entity, 'change', generationId, bitflag, target);
        if (match) query.add(entity);
        else query.remove(world, entity);
    }

    return data;
}

export function setChanged(world: World, entity: Entity, trait: Trait) {
    const data = markChanged(world, entity, trait);
    if (!data) return;
    for (const sub of data.changeSubscriptions) sub(entity);
}

export function setPairChanged(world: World, entity: Entity, trait: Trait, target: Entity) {
    const data = markChanged(world, entity, trait, target);
    if (!data) return;

    // Find the relation to capture per-target data for iteration.
    const relation = trait[$internal].relation;
    const pairData = relation ? getRelationData(world, entity, relation, target) : undefined;
    logPairEvent(world, trait, entity, target, 'change', pairData);

    for (const sub of data.changeSubscriptions) sub(entity, target);
}

import { $internal } from '../../common';
import { isRelationPair } from '../../relation/utils/is-relation';
import type { Relation } from '../../relation/types';
import type { Trait } from '../../trait/types';
import { isModifier } from '../modifier';
import type { QueryHash, QueryParameter } from '../types';

const sortedIDs = new Float64Array(1024); // Use Float64 for larger IDs with relation encoding

export const createQueryHash = (parameters: QueryParameter[]): QueryHash => {
    sortedIDs.fill(0);
    let cursor = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < parameters.length; i++) {
        const param = parameters[i];

        if (isRelationPair(param)) {
            // Encode relation pair as: (relationTraitId * 1000000) + targetId
            // This ensures unique hashes for different relation/target combinations
            const pairCtx = param[$internal];
            const relation = pairCtx.relation;
            const target = pairCtx.target;

            const relationId = (relation as Relation<Trait>)[$internal].trait.id;
            const targetId = typeof target === 'number' ? target : -1;

            // Combine into a unique hash number
            sortedIDs[cursor++] = relationId * 10000000 + targetId + 5000000;
        } else if (isModifier(param)) {
            const modifierId = param.id;
            const traitIds = param.traitIds;

            for (let i = 0; i < traitIds.length; i++) {
                const traitId = traitIds[i];
                sortedIDs[cursor++] = modifierId * 100000 + traitId;
            }
        } else {
            const traitId = (param as Trait).id;
            sortedIDs[cursor++] = traitId;
        }
    }

    // Sort only the portion of the array that has been filled.
    const filledArray = sortedIDs.subarray(0, cursor);
    filledArray.sort();

    // Create string key.
    const hash = filledArray.join(',');

    return hash;
};

Now the big part: query-result.ts per-target data resolution. Let me rewrite `getQueryStores`, `createSnapshots`, `createSnapshotsWithAtomic`, and
updateEach commit logic to support pair resolvers.

Let me design:
- Add a parallel array `pairResolvers: (PairResolver | undefined)[]` where `PairResolver = { relation: Relation<Trait>; target: Entity }`.
- `getQueryStores` fills `pairResolvers` alongside traits/stores.

For modifier params:
- If modifier has pairs, iterate pairs first: for each pair, if pair target is specific number and base trait non-tag, push baseTrait+store and a
resolver with relation+target.
- Then iterate modifier.traits, skipping traits that are already covered by a pair (dedupe by trait id). Actually if `Added(Likes(alice),
Likes(bob))`, modifier.traits = [Likes, Likes]; pairs = [Likes(alice), Likes(bob)]. If I push pairs first and skip all Likes traits, I get two entries
(alice, bob) — good. If `Added(Likes(alice), Position)`, traits = [Likes, Position], pairs=[Likes(alice)]. Push pair (Likes/alice), then skip Likes,
push Position. Good.
- For a pair with wildcard target `'*'`, data resolution doesn't make sense (which target?). We'll skip wildcard pairs in query result (or resolve
undefined). Actually `Added(Likes('*'))` iteration — what data should be exposed? There may be multiple targets. Probably the base trait store makes
sense, or per-target for whichever changed. Hard to know. Let me skip wildcard pairs from resolvers and instead fall back to base trait store for
them? But if we skip, and the trait is only in pairs, it won't be in traits at all → no data slot. Let me handle wildcard pairs by pushing base
trait+store with resolver target undefined (i.e., raw base trait). That gives `{ amount: [...] }` which is at least something. Fine.

Actually, let me think: for wildcard pair `Added(Likes('*'))`, the user can't know which target from the data slot, but the query returns entities;
they can use `entity.targetsFor(Likes)`. The data as base trait store is acceptable. For per-target resolution, wildcard can't resolve to one target.
So for wildcard, push raw base trait (no resolver). Let me implement: only pairs with specific number targets get resolver; wildcard pairs fall back
to raw base trait.

- For top-level relation pair params (not modifiers): currently pushes baseTrait+store. Should we resolve per-target? The task says "Query result
iteration resolves per-target relation data for pair-tracked traits" — specifically pair-tracked traits (modifiers). Top-level relation pair queries
use fast path `createRelationOnlyQueryResult` anyway (for single pair). For combined queries like `Removed(Contains), Contains(gold)`, the top-level
`Contains(gold)` could also benefit from per-target resolution. The existing test expects `contains.amount === 42`. If we add resolver for top-level
pair too, then `Removed(Contains), Contains(gold)` — getQueryStores would push Contains twice: once for top-level pair (resolver gold), once for
modifier `Removed(Contains)` (no pair, so raw). Then readEach gives `[42, {amount:[]}]` — the callback destructures `([contains])` which is first slot
= 42 via resolver. That would make the existing test actually meaningful (42)! Let me add resolver for top-level relation pairs too. But if the pair
is removed (not present), getRelationData returns undefined; fallback to global event data via getPairTrackedData gives 42.

But wait, for `world.query(Contains(gold))` single pair, the fast path returns `createRelationOnlyQueryResult` with no data slots — readEach gives
`[]`. That's existing behavior; we won't change fast path.

Let me now design `getQueryStores` signature: currently `(params, traits, stores, world)`. I'll add a `pairResolvers` array param. It's called from
`createQueryResult` and `select`. Let me update both.

Define type:
```ts
type PairResolver = {
    relation: Relation<Trait>;
    target: Entity;
};
```

`createSnapshots` and `createSnapshotsWithAtomic`:
```ts
function createSnapshots(world, entity, traits, stores, state, pairResolvers) {
    const eid = getEntityId(entity);
    for (let i = 0; i < traits.length; i++) {
        const resolver = pairResolvers[i];
        const trait = traits[i];
        const ctx = trait[$internal];
        if (resolver) {
            state[i] = getPairTrackedData(world, entity, resolver.relation, resolver.target);
        } else {
            state[i] = ctx.get(eid, stores[i]);
        }
    }
}
```

`createSnapshotsWithAtomic`: similar but also atomic snapshot for AoS. For pair resolver, atomic snapshot = `{...value}` if object.

Now updateEach commit:
- For tracked indices: if resolver present, compare old/new via shallowEqual, and if changed, `setRelationData(world, entity, resolver.relation,
resolver.target, newValue)` and push changedPair with target? Actually `setChanged` currently called for regular trait changes. For pair changes, we
should call `setPairChanged`. But updateEach in query-result collects `[entity, trait]` and calls `setChanged`. For resolver entries, we need to call
`setPairChanged` with target. Let me collect `[entity, trait, target?]` triples and dispatch accordingly.
- For untracked indices: if resolver present, `setRelationData(world, entity, relation, target, newValue)`; else `ctx.fastSet`.

For 'always' and 'never' paths similarly.

support. I'll keep the existing structure but add `pairResolvers`.

Let me carefully write the new file. Key pieces:
- `PairResolver` type
- `getQueryStores` fills traits, stores, pairResolvers
- `createSnapshots` / `createSnapshotsWithAtomic` accept world, entity, pairResolvers
- updateEach commit loops handle resolvers
- readEach uses new snapshot signature
- select resets pairResolvers too

For commit:
- `changedPairs: [Entity, Trait, Entity?][]`
- For resolver in tracked: `changed = !shallowEqual(newValue, oldState)`; if changed, setRelationData(world, entity, relation, target, newValue) and
push `[entity, trait, target]`.
- For resolver in untracked: setRelationData.
- For 'always': same as tracked for all.
- For 'never': for resolver, setRelationData; else fastSet.
- Trigger: for each changed pair, if target !== undefined → setPairChanged(world, entity, trait, target) else setChanged(world, entity, trait).

But note: for a removed pair (pair not present), `setRelationData` would find targetIndex -1 and no-op. Fine.

Let me also handle `useStores` — it returns the raw stores, which for pair-tracked traits would be base stores (array form). That's acceptable;
`useStores` is for low-level store access.
